24/06/21, 10:49 - Persona: As agreed with @Persona , we need to change the policy in the Whatsapp chat a little, new rules shall apply:
Issues reported by Azienda shall not by fixed by SSI if Azienda can self resolve it. If Azienda is not capable to do SSI can highlight (e.g. check dialogue COD) 
SSI shall not quick fix something because it's faster and easier for the daily operation. It's OK to invest 20 minutes for checking and Azienda is subsequently independent.
Issues reported by Azienda that are a real OIL shall be treated as an OIL (Excel, ITS, JIRA).
Main target is to get Azienda into the possibility to be independent, the chat will get inactivated sooner or later. 
Thank you for your cooperation at both sides.
